# Critic Verdicts

> Append-only. The independent non-author critic's verdict per unit (RFC0001 D3).
> APPROVE = ready; REJECT = repair before Done. Latest row per unit wins.
> Reviewer must differ from Author - a self-review never clears the Done gate.
> The `pre-gate` author marks units closed before the independence gate existed
> (under the prior risk-scaled policy that permitted light-tier self-review).

## Errata

An append-only log earns its authority by never being quietly rewritten, so a correction is
recorded here rather than made invisibly. Written as prose, not a table: the row parser reads
every pipe-delimited line in this file, and an errata TABLE is parsed as malformed verdicts.

One row has been removed since adoption.

- **2026-07-20, US0276.** An `APPROVE` row naming `Darren Benson (operator)` as REVIEWER, with
  author `sdlc-studio; agent; v1`, was removed. The event it recorded did not happen: the operator
  was the reviewer of RECORD (the principal who signs off), not the adversarial critic. The critic
  pass was run by an independent subagent, and that row remains. The wrong row was written by the
  author in error. Because the sign-off gate refuses a principal who is a recorded reviewer on the
  same unit, it permanently blocked the legitimate sign-off - so leaving it would have both
  stranded the unit and misreported who reviewed what. Removed with the operator's explicit
  authorisation. CR0372 tracks giving this a `supersede` command, since hand-editing an
  append-only log is exactly what it exists to prevent.

Residual, non-blocking: within an open run the batch can only grow, so deliberately narrowing scope over-reports rather than omits - the correct trade (a named-but-dismissed item is a shrug; a silently-dropped one is trusted). | US0272 | APPROVE | independent-record-reviewer; agent; subagent (narrow brief: does the retrospective story describe SHIPPED behaviour) | sdlc-studio; agent; v1 | 2026-07-20 | - | - | APPROVE. Ran all three Verify commands and re-derived each claim independently rather than trusting the story. AC1's test asserts findings are non-empty AND all warn, so it cannot pass by silently dropping the placeholder. AC2 verified across the whole type/status matrix: story Ready/In Progress/Done and epic, bug, cr all error; only story Proposed/Draft warn; a missing status falls through to error rather than defaulting open - the relaxation does not leak. AC3 verified independently by constructing a Ready placeholder-only story in a clean repo: conformance exits 1 with missing decomposed, specified, verifiable. Chain honest: EP0088 Done with derived point total 2 matching US0272, US0272 the only child, CR0342 Complete via Decomposed-into, all index rows agree with the bodies. OVERCLAIM PROBE: transition set --status Ready SUCCEEDS on a pure-placeholder story, so anything claiming the placeholder blocks that transition is false. The STORY does not claim it - AC3 says moved to Ready, consistent with the move succeeding - but two shipped comments in validate.py did; both corrected in this commit. Three findings raised: Affects under-recorded conformance.py though AC3's own test targets it (fixed); the User Story block was still placeholders at Done (fixed); validate emits severity warn while its counters count only warning, so three printed WARN lines report as warnings=0 (reproduced independently, filed BG0217). |

Repaired and re-verified - all three ACs now fail when their subject is removed. |

| US0461 | APPROVE | Darren Benson (operator, reviewer of record) | Claude Opus 5 | 2026-07-30 | - | - | - This is the REVIEWER OF RECORD's approval of the CLOSE, not a clean adversarial pass. Five independent adversarial reviewers returned REJECT on all 11 units (14 MAJOR findings); their verdicts and findings are in critic-evidence.md and are not superseded by this row. - Four MAJORs that had reached the tracked tree are repaired and committed: BG0426 (the epic story census could not read the shipped template's linked Epic field, so a story count of 7 was written where the true count is 18), BG0427 (three defects in the epic-index sweep), BG0428 (the supersession waiver key and a phantom waived pair). - Eleven findings are CARRIED as known issues on the operator's stated rule - close the stories, raise bugs for the parts that fail, and if none are stop-ships carry them for a future sprint: BG0429-BG0439, 26 points, each with an executed reproduction. - BG0436 is named as the closest to a stop-ship: resolve\_affects never consults the installed skill dir despite its docstring, so detector-owed --file raises an uncaught traceback on any project that did not vendor the skill in-tree. It needs an owed lens plus --file to fire, which is why it is carried rather than held, and it is first in the next queue. - The process failure is filed separately: CR0503 (the seat review ceremony is compulsory in prose and was bypassed on this very batch), CR0504 (five defect classes account for all 14 findings, none of which needed a reviewer to find), CR0505 (a sprint has no compulsory checklist or report document). - Every unit's executable acceptance criteria pass. US0484's AC3 was caught RED at the close by the Done gate - its selector named a class that does not hold the test - and the selector is corrected.; BLOCKING: none. The close is approved with the known issues accepted, not resolved. |

| BG0442 | REJECT | engineering seat (independent, isolated worktree, closing full-diff review) | Claude Opus 5 | 2026-07-31 | - | - | BG0442 AC3's verifier is satisfied by a hardcoded constant in the very function the bug is about: replacing 'outside = \_findings\_outside\_batches(root, spans)' at sprint.py:4587 with the literal 2 SURVIVED all 623 tests of test\_sprint.py and its own verifier in isolation - the one true survivor of 54 mutants. The bug's own AC2 names this hazard verbatim but places the control on the helper, not on the rendering path AC3 covers. BG0452 declares handoff.py and test\_handoff.py in Affects and its Summary names handoff.py:711 as carrying the identical defect, but neither file was touched and the line still read a split on the first hyphen, so a v3 HO-<ulid>-slug key never resolved; AC3 substituted a fourth site the bug never named, and provenance.py:97,130 carried the same pattern. BOTH REPAIRED under BG0465, each verified by replaying the reviewer's own mutant. US0570: the row stopped reading the retro instead of the plan, but a planned unit that was HELD and absent from the retro was emitted under BOTH headings, so one undelivered unit rendered '1 held, 1 UNACCOUNTED' beside '1/2 unit(s)' - the arithmetic the row exists to make readable, stating two problems where there was one. US0572: the authoriser limb held, but scope\_tail\_error stripped the scope tail while the subject normaliser stripped only the outer ends, so the VALIDATOR was more permissive than the STORE - 'rule:sprint-checklist: cost' passed, was written with the space intact, and the lookup never found it, so the waiver recorded cleanly, printed success, read as accepted and covered nothing while the item stayed outstanding. Verbatim the defect the scope check had just been added to end, reintroduced one layer down by the check itself; subjects are now normalised segment by segment. The seat also found three repairs behaviourally correct but pinned by NOTHING - the UNACCOUNTED bucket, the carried-table exception limb and the planned-points absent-versus-zero sentinel each survived deletion - and all three now have discriminating tests. US0572: the authoriser limb held, but scope\_tail\_error stripped the scope tail while the subject normaliser stripped only the outer ends, so the VALIDATOR was more permissive than the STORE - 'rule:sprint-checklist: cost' passed, was written with the space intact, and the lookup never found it, so the waiver recorded cleanly, printed success, read as accepted and covered nothing while the item stayed outstanding. Verbatim the defect the scope check had just been added to end, reintroduced one layer down by the check itself; subjects are now normalised segment by segment. The seat also found three repairs behaviourally correct but pinned by NOTHING - the UNACCOUNTED bucket, the carried-table exception limb and the planned-points absent-versus-zero sentinel each survived deletion - and all three now have discriminating tests. Six mutants over the seat's findings, all six killed. |
